function EnableESP(...)
	if j then
		return;
	end;
	j = true;
	for E, t in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		createESP(t);
	end;
	task[yk(3397068358 % 15727019)](function(...)
		while j do
			pcall(updateESP);
			task[yk(-924965 - (-957879))](I);
		end;
	end);
end;
function DisableESP(...)
	j = false;
	for E, t in pairs(H) do
		removeESP(E);
	end;
	H = {};
end;

function getClosestPlayer(E, ...)
	local t = player[yk(-987916 - (-1020516))] and player[yk(695556632 % 8280048)]:FindFirstChild(yk(288279 + -255669));
	if not t then
		return nil;
	end;
	local N = E or soruMaxDist or 485786620 % 3094160;
	if AimlockTargetPlayer ~= yk(14076 + 18150) and AimlockTargetPlayer ~= nil then
		local E = Players:FindFirstChild(AimlockTargetPlayer);
		if E and (E[yk(572264 + -539664)] and E[yk(-107040 - (-139640))]:FindFirstChild(yk(2907516756 % 15383514))) then
			local t = E[yk(217306 + -184706)]:FindFirstChildOfClass(yk(997569 + -965376));
			if t and t[yk(-515953 + 548345)] > 69561030 % 2107910 then
				return E[yk(475270997 % 4281427)];
			end;
		end;
	end;
	local b, C = nil, math[yk(770017 - 737127)];
	for E, a in pairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		if a ~= player and (not BlacklistedPlayers[a[yk(753732 - 720841)]] and (a[yk(290645208 % 5588704)] and a[yk(242567 - 209967)]:FindFirstChild(yk(-63674 + 96284)))) then
			local E = a[yk(621172 - 588572)]:FindFirstChildOfClass(yk(243033 - 210840));
			if E and E[yk(113799 + -81407)] > -844420 + 844420 then
				local E = ((a[yk(-157676 - (-190276))][yk(915037 - 882427)][yk(259668 + -227532)] - t[yk(-476856 - (-508992))]))[yk(149701633 % 5345321)];
				if E < C and E <= N then
					C = E;
					b = a[yk(1346051800 % 10515775)];
				end;
			end;
		end;
	end;
	return b;
end;

end;

				if string[yk(689922 + -657452)](b[yk(391017 + -358126)]) == string[yk(-88203 + 120673)](E) or string[yk(452428 + -420338)](string[yk(57155 - 24685)](b[yk(283153931 % 14156052)]), string[yk(1209660030 % 4957490)](E)) then

		end;
	end;

end;

		local E = (currentLang == yk(318002328 % 1276987));
		for t, N in ipairs(I0) do

function updateLangBtnColors(...)
	local E = isColorLight(x);
	local t = Color3[yk(1265482344 % 7716160)](505436 + -505421, -92224 - (-92234), 46922249 % 15640743);
	local N = Color3[yk(-826714 - (-858818))](112404 - 112149, -909681 + 909936, 198198 + -197943);
	if currentLang == yk(937975 - 905410) then
		jk[yk(385300981 % 16052857)] = x;
		jk[yk(184866 + -152655)] = E and t or N;
		lk[yk(-844622 + 877035)] = S[yk(1050613 - 1018250)];
		lk[yk(341047 - 308836)] = N;
		if not table[yk(658881 + -626791)](Y, jk) then
			table[yk(757651 - 725065)](Y, jk);
		end;
		local b = table[yk(926365 - 894275)](Y, lk);
		if b then
			table[yk(196362 + -163514)](Y, b);
		end;
	else
		lk[yk(978431938 % 13780275)] = x;
		lk[yk(50018 + -17807)] = E and t or N;
		jk[yk(351618 - 319205)] = S[yk(-70025 - (-102388))];
